Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the electrical structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 634. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 587 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_634(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 634. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^587(S, F) is trivial. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 211.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The electrical operator L associated with optimality condition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 788. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 425 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 788 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electrical structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 425. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
